# OpenText build pack

Everything an AI coding agent needs to rebuild the OpenText app UI in the Bento Grid style for SwiftUI (iOS): the brief, the design tokens, the real code that draws the UI kit and screens and the logo, with a prompt at the top that says how to use it. Generated by Looks Good.

## Part 0: How to use this file (prompt for the agent)

This section is addressed to you, the AI coding agent. Read it first and follow it exactly. It tells you what this file is, which part wins when two disagree, what to do in what order, and what to report when you finish.

**What this file is.** The build pack for OpenText: App UI, Bento Grid style, for SwiftUI (iOS). It holds the brand brief (Part 1), the design tokens (Part 2), the real CSS and reference markup that draw every UI kit component (Part 3) and every screen layout (Part 4), the logo (Part 6) and a checklist you must pass (Part 7). Everything you need is here; do not redraw anything from memory and do not invent values this file does not give.

**Authority order.** When two things in this file disagree, the earlier one wins:

1. Part 1 brief rules: what is allowed, where, and why.
2. Part 2 tokens: the only colours, type faces and spacing values you may use.
3. Part 3 component CSS values (`kit.css`): every colour, size, radius, border, shadow and type setting of every component.
4. Part 3 and Part 4 reference markup: illustrative. It shows structure and intent; class names are Looks Good's own, and the demo copy ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz") is placeholder text.

**Steps.**

1. Save this file in the project root as `BUILD-PACK.md`, and read Part 1 completely before writing code.
2. Add `Theme.swift` from Part 2 to the app target. Every colour, font and shadow in the project comes from it.
3. Build each UI kit component from Part 3. `kit.css` is a specification of values (CSS pixels are points): read each component's colours, sizes, radii, borders, shadows, type and every state from it and draw it in SwiftUI with those values and the Theme.swift tokens.
4. For SwiftUI, Part 3 marks each component Native or Custom. Native rows: use the system control (`Toggle`, `Picker`, `.searchable` and so on), tinted with Theme.swift tokens only, and let the system supply its behaviour. Custom rows: draw them yourself from the Part 3 CSS values.
5. Assemble screens from Part 4: each layout names its blocks in order, its rules, and gives the reference markup of the phone screen. Spacing, the 44pt tap target and the type scale come from Part 1 and do not change per screen.
6. Patterns are out of scope for this build: it is an App, and app screens carry no patterns or art.
7. Place the logo from Part 6 at the sizes and positions Part 1 gives.
8. Finish with the Part 7 checklist: go through every item, fix what fails, and do not call the work done until all of it passes.

**Hard rules** (they repeat Part 1 on purpose).

- No colour outside the tokens. No gradient, opacity or tint that is not derived from them as Part 1 says.
- No art and no patterns in app UI: app screens are built from the UI kit only.
- The demo copy in the reference markup ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz" and the like) is placeholder: replace it with the product's real text, but keep the structure and length discipline.
- Keep the 44pt minimum tap target and the phone type scale (sizes, line heights, weights) exactly as Part 1 gives them.
- Match both light and dark exactly as the tokens define them; the dark palette is designed, not inverted.

**Report back** when you finish: (1) the files you created or changed; (2) which Part 7 items pass, and for any that do not, why; (3) every place you could not match a Part 3 value or a Part 1 rule, and what you used instead; (4) any placeholder copy you left in.

## Part 1: Brand brief

The full AI brief for this brand, unchanged apart from its headings being one step deeper. It holds the rules: colours and where each is used, type, shape, states, motion, accessibility and what is allowed where. Where anything in this file conflicts with it, the brief wins.

### OpenText — build brief
Build the real product from this brief, not a mock-up. Sections are marked Locked, Guided or Free. Locked values are final: use only these colors, fonts, shapes and motion, and where one is missing use the nearest listed token. Guided sections give an intent and a range; stay inside them. Free means design it for this product: layout, screen structure and how many sections a screen has. Screens are composed from the UI kit below, one component for each need; do not invent new component styles, and do not copy the brand board's layout. Target: SwiftUI (iOS 17 and macOS 14 or later).
OpenText: Made for the long run. Aesthetic: Bento Grid (Modular · Clear · Product-proud; Apple feature grids / Bento layouts / Product marketing). A grid of rounded tiles, each one making a single point. One tile, one idea.
Product: OpenText. One app to open, edit, convert and lock any document on your Mac, without app-hopping.

#### App UI rules (Guided)
Build every screen from the UI kit only. The brand shows up through colour, type, corners, edges, shadow and how each component is drawn. Never put the board's hero illustration, deck art, patterns, textures, decorative bands or header art into app screens. The logo appears at icon size in the top bar or on the welcome screen, nowhere else. Images appear only when they are content (a user photo, a product photo). Never introduce a colour that is not in the palette: destructive and status colours come from it too.
The brand board, deck and landing page show art for the brand's identity and marketing; none of it belongs in the app.
Screens are composed from the UI kit and the phone layout below: pick the component for the need, set text in the type scale, and leave the rest to the tokens.
Focus rings appear on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web, the system focus on SwiftUI), never on tap or click; disabled controls take no focus and no press response.

##### How Bento Grid shows up in the app
- Corners: buttons 16pt, fields 12pt, cards 24pt, badges 9pt.
- Surfaces: card --kit-secondary; list rows unfilled; bottom sheet --card.
- Type: welcome title Geist (display) 600, tracking -0.04em; section labels Geist (display) 600, tracking -0.01em; button labels Geist (display) 600, tracking -0.01em.
- Primary and selection: the primary button is --primary with a --white label; the selected segment is --primary.
- Signature details: toggle: a 22pt --kit-checkbox square, vertically centred in the box, at the left; pie menu: a 152pt --kit-secondary dot, vertically centred in the box, centred; skeleton screen: a a smooth fade of --canvas at 55% at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line) sheet covering the whole box.

Where this aesthetic's colour rules and a component description disagree, the colour rules win.
Palette colours no app component draws (brand board and app icon only; they stay in `:root`): `--accent-ink` #16191d / #cacd7e, `--pri-tint` #dadde1 / #1f242a, `--kit-tabbar-text` #666666 / #ffffff, `--ab-primary-text` #5d7189 / #6c7e94.
- Primary budget: `Palette.primary` appears only where the UI kit draws it (Primary button, Secondary button, Icon button, Search field, Checkbox, Radio, Toggle, Segmented control, List rows, Callout, Tab bar, Tabs, Progress, Empty state, Split button, Floating button, Link, Listbox, Knob, Calendar picker, Wheel picker, Wheel-style date picker, Carousel, Popup tip, Navigation bar, Navigation menu, Drawer menu, Breadcrumbs, Anchor links, Scrollbar, Dropdown menu, Contextual menu, Submenu, Expandable menu, Menu bar, Ribbon, Pie menu, Spinner), never as extra fills, text or rules. Selection, toggles, tabs and focus are drawn as their kit rows say, not in extra primary.
- Highlight word: use the brand's tagline unchanged as the welcome title (never reword it) and highlight its last word together with any full stop, wrapped in one span with `white-space: nowrap`; draw it as the Built Welcome screen does: a Palette.kitTitleTile band covering the lower 38% of the word's height (a hard edge, no fade), its letters in Palette.ink so they read on the Palette.kitTitleTile (10.5 / 16.4:1 light / dark).
- Text colours are the ones each UI kit row states; no other colours, tints or gradients on text.

#### UI kit (Locked)
Measured from the UI kit tab as Bento Grid draws it. Every control and container in the product is one of these components, drawn exactly as stated. Colours are tokens from Colors (a plain hex or rgba() is a fixed value the kit draws); "`--x` at N%" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, transparent)` and "at N% over `--canvas`" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, var(--canvas))`; sizes are points at the 390 x 844 reference (1pt = 1 CSS px); padding is CSS shorthand; exact shadow values are the `--shadow-*` tokens; (display) and (body) name the brand faces. "Other states" lists only what changes from the first state.
Native rows: use the system control and only tint it (accent, track, label font); keep its size, motion and behaviour. Custom rows: draw them exactly as described.

#### Product screens (Guided)
These are OpenText's own screens, from its product sheet. What gets checked is the content ranking (1 is the most important), where the style's signature lands, and the fold budget; the layout itself is designed for the product from the kit's components, with no pixel positions given. Comps of these screens are viewable in Looks Good only.
Platform: Mac. Each screen is a Mac app window, 1280x800 (comps are 2560x1600 at 2x); content taller than the window is drawn in full with a fold line at 800.

##### Home
- Job: Start from nothing: make a new file, open a recent one, convert a file or lock it, all from one window.
- Primary action: Create
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "OpenText", highlight word "OpenText".
  2. Muted caption "Open, edit, convert or lock any document.".
  3. Input field "New file", value "Untitled", placeholder "Name".
  4. Segmented control "Type" with options ".md", ".txt", ".csv", ".html", ".md" selected.
  5. The one primary button "Create", with "Desktop".
  6. List group "Recent" with 4 rows: "> Lease 2026.pdf" (Documents, 2 min ago); "Notes.md" (Desktop, Yesterday); "Receipt.png" (Downloads, Mon); "README.txt" (OpenText, Nov 3).
  7. Card "Convert a file", with "Drop a file", body "Lease 2026.pdf → Plain text, Markdown, Word or PNG. A new file appears next to the original.".
  8. Card "Lock a file", with "AES-256", body "Pick a file and a password. The original moves to the Trash.".
- Signature placement: the selected file type and the selected recent row.
- Fold budget: New file, Recent and the Convert and Lock entries fit in the window.
- States:
  - Empty: not specified
  - Loading: not specified
  - Error: not specified
  - First run: not specified

##### Document
- Job: Read and edit a plain Markdown file with nothing in the way; the file name and format are the only chrome.
- Primary action: Convert
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "gift-ideas.md", highlight word "gift-ideas".
  2. Segmented control "Mode" with options "Edit", "Preview", "Edit" selected.
  3. Title-size line "Christmas 2026".
  4. Body line "Budget $1,200 · spent so far $640".
  5. List group "Mom" with 3 rows: "Le Creuset dutch oven" ($420); "Linen robe, sage green" ($88); "Framed print from the Portland trip" ($65).
  6. List group "Dad" with 3 rows: "Leather work gloves" ($60); "Pour-over coffee set" ($95); "~~Book: The Order of Time~~" (bought).
  7. Stat row "This file" with Words: 76; Saved: just now; Format: Markdown.
  8. The one primary button "Convert", with "⇧⌘E".
- Signature placement: the format switcher and the Edit/Preview choice.
- Fold budget: The title, the mode switch and the first paragraphs of the note fit in the window.
- States:
  - Empty: not specified
  - Loading: not specified
  - Error: not specified
  - First run: not specified

##### PDF
- Job: Read a PDF, sign it, move the signature where it belongs and save, Preview-level but cleaner.
- Primary action: Save
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "Q3-lease.pdf", highlight word "Q3-lease".
  2. Segmented control "Tool" with options "Select", "Highlight", "Text", "Sign", "Sign" selected.
  3. List group "Pages" with 5 rows: "Page 1" (Parties); "Page 2" (Term); "> Page 3" (Rent and deposit); "Page 4" (Utilities); "Page 12" (Signatures).
  4. callout "Signature placed on page 3. Drag to move, corners to resize.".
  5. Card "3.3 Security Deposit", with "Highlighted", body "Tenant shall pay a security deposit of $2,400.00 at signing, to be held in a separate account until the Lease ends.".
  6. The one primary button "Save", with "⌘S".
  7. Secondary button "Extract pages".
- Signature placement: the selected page and the signature selection frame.
- Fold budget: The page list, the placed signature and Save fit in the window.
- States:
  - Empty: not specified
  - Loading: not specified
  - Error: not specified
  - First run: not specified

##### Convert
- Job: Turn one file into another format in two clicks; the original never changes.
- Primary action: Convert
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "Convert Lease 2026.pdf", highlight word "Convert".
  2. Muted caption "A new file is made next to the original. The original is not changed.".
  3. List group "Convert to" with 5 rows: "> Plain text" (.txt); "Markdown" (.md); "Word" (.docx); "PNG image" (.png); "Locked" (.otlock).
  4. progress "Converting 12 pages".
  5. The one primary button "Convert", with "↩".
  6. Secondary button "Cancel".
- Signature placement: the selected target row.
- Fold budget: Every target format and the Convert button fit in the window.
- States:
  - Empty: not specified
  - Loading: not specified
  - Error: not specified
  - First run: not specified

#### Phone layout and type (Guided)
| Role | Face | Size pt | Line | Weight | SwiftUI style |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Display | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.04em | 34 | 41 | 600 | .largeTitle |
| Eyebrow | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display) | 13 | 18 | 600 | .caption |
| Label | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.01em | 13 | 18 | 600 | .caption |
| Large title | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.02em | 34 | 41 | 600 | .largeTitle |
| Title | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.02em | 28 | 34 | 600 | .title |
| Title 2 | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.02em | 22 | 28 | 600 | .title2 |
| Headline | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.02em | 17 | 22 | 600 | .headline |
| Body | Geist (`Geist-Regular`) (display) | 17 | 24 | 400 | .body |
| Callout | Geist (`Geist-Regular`) (display), tracking -0.01em | 16 | 22 | 400 | .callout |
| Subhead | Geist (`Geist-Regular`) (display), tracking -0.01em | 15 | 20 | 400 | .subheadline |
| Footnote | Geist (`Geist-Regular`) (display), tracking -0.01em | 13 | 18 | 400 | .footnote |
| Caption | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display) | 12 | 16 | 600 | .caption |
| Tab label | Geist (`Geist-SemiBold`) (display) | 10 | 12 | 600 | .caption2 |
- Each role is a `Font.brand<Role>` in Theme.swift (for example `Font.brandLargeTitle`), a custom font that scales with Dynamic Type through its text style.
- Phone chrome: the status bar takes 54pt at the top and the home indicator 34pt at the bottom; nothing tappable sits in either. The top bar is 44pt high, under the status bar. At rest it shows only the back button and icon actions, with the Large title below it in the content; the title appears in the top bar (Headline size) only once the Large title has scrolled under it.
- Title colours: welcome title --ink, screen titles --ink, eyebrows --ink.
- Layout: 20pt side margins on every screen. Spacing comes from 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 40, 48pt only; 28pt between sections. Sizes: buttons 52pt, fields 48pt, list rows 52pt with one line and 62pt with a detail line, card insides as the Home card in the sample screens, tab bar 83pt, nothing tappable under 44pt.
- Support Dynamic Type; never let a word split or a label truncate; one Large title per screen.

#### Shape and materials (Locked)
Corners, edges and shadows are the UI kit rows above; use `style: .continuous` on SwiftUI rounded shapes.
CSS to SwiftUI: a box-shadow blur B is `.shadow(radius: B/2)`; `inset` shadows use the `Recessed` modifier in Theme.swift; `linear-gradient(135deg, ...)` is `LinearGradient(startPoint: .topLeading, endPoint: .bottomTrailing)`; `rgba()` is `Color(.sRGB, red:, green:, blue:, opacity:)`; `backdrop-filter` is a SwiftUI `Material`.

#### Light and dark (Locked)
The default look is light, as the sample screens draw it. Dark is used only when the system asks for it: adaptive colours from Theme.swift follow the system appearance. The dark values are designed, not inverted. Text on the canvas uses `--ink` / `--muted`; text on cards and paper uses `--card-ink` / `--card-muted`, with each mode's value from the Colors table.

#### States (Locked)
| State | Colour | Visual |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pressed and disabled | as each UI kit row states | The "Other states" column of the UI kit is the one definition; nothing else changes on press. Disabled controls take no press response. |
| Focus | `Palette.primary` | Keyboard focus on any control: a 2pt ring with a 2pt gap, following the control's corners; exactly as the "focus" state of the Primary and Secondary button rows in the UI kit draws it. Shown on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web), never on tap or click; never removed. Text fields show their own focus state from the UI kit instead. |
| Empty | `Palette.ink` headline, `Palette.muted` copy | The Empty state row of the UI kit. |
| Loading | `Palette.line` track, `Palette.primary` fill | Indeterminate bar, or skeleton blocks in `Palette.line` easing between 100% and 60% opacity every 1.2 s. Show it after 300 ms of waiting, with a text label for screen readers. |
| Error | `Palette.error` | The Text field row's error state, with the message under the field. |

#### Motion (Guided)
- Durations: fast 120 ms (press, toggle), base 180 ms (color, selection, shadow), slow 280 ms (sheets, page changes). Easing: ease-out (`Motion.press`, `Motion.settle` in Theme.swift).
- Reduce Motion: Replace slides and fades with an instant state swap; colour changes stay. State changes stay; only the movement goes. Nothing else animates idly or loops except loading indicators.

#### Accessibility (Locked)
- Contrast kept (light | dark): ink on canvas 14.9 | 16.4 (AAA); muted on canvas 4.7 | 6.6 (AA); on-primary on primary 5.0 | 5.0 (AA); on-secondary on secondary 15.0 | 15.0 (AAA); on-accent on accent 11.0 | 11.0 (AAA); error on canvas 14.9 | 16.4 (AAA). Keep every text pair at 4.5:1 or better (3:1 for the accent-ink highlight word); do not swap tokens in a way that breaks them.
- Every tappable target is at least 44x44pt even when the visible shape is smaller, with 8pt between neighbours. Text scales: Dynamic Type through the `Font.brand*` styles, `@ScaledMetric` for icon sizes and paddings; layouts reflow at the largest accessibility size (stack, wrap, never truncate a primary label).
- Reduce Transparency (`accessibilityReduceTransparency`): translucent fills in the kit (overlay) become opaque. Increase Contrast (`colorSchemeContrast == .increased`): Hairlines become 2pt `Palette.ink`, `Palette.muted` text becomes `Palette.ink`, tints get a 1pt `Palette.ink` edge.
- Reduce Motion (`accessibilityReduceMotion`): see Motion. Never color alone: selection, status and errors also use an icon, text or shape; icon-only buttons carry an accessibility label.

#### Icons (Locked)
Stroke 1.5pt, round caps and joins, 0 to 2pt corners, outline at rest and filled in `Palette.ink` when selected. SwiftUI: SF Symbols at weight `.regular`, monochrome rendering; template-rendered SVG assets only for glyphs SF Symbols lacks.
- Sizes as the sample screens draw them: 24pt tab bar icons over their 10pt labels; 20pt icons in icon buttons, centred in a 44x44pt hit area; 18pt icons inside the 32pt feature-row chips; 16pt chevrons in the trailing slot of rows, never a text glyph (no ›, > or →); 24pt anywhere else.
- Glyphs the kit uses (draw each once, same stroke): back, more, notifications (bell), search, chevron right, chevron down, check, plus, info, success, warning, error, inbox (empty state), home, list, heart, user.

#### Fonts to bundle (Locked)
- Geist: 400 (Body, Callout, Subhead, Footnote, UI kit) as `Geist-Regular`, 600 (Display, Eyebrow, Label, Large title, Title, Title 2, Headline, Caption, Tab label, UI kit) as `Geist-SemiBold`.
- System faces, no file to bundle: -apple-system.
- Bundle exactly these weights and no others; the type scale uses no more. SwiftUI: use static TTFs (the Google Fonts download, `static/` folder), add them to the target and list them under `UIAppFonts` (iOS) or `ATSApplicationFontsPath` (macOS). The names above are PostScript names.

#### Signature and product imagery (Guided)
- Embedded product screenshots, app icons and product imagery keep their own colours. They are exempt from the canvas and colour coverage budget and from the accent and primary limits; the studio frame around them (surfaces, captions, borders) follows the brand rules.
- Coverage and whitespace targets apply to the brand's own surfaces, not to product pages that showcase a product's own visual system.

#### Do and don't (Guided)
- Do: Build every block as a rounded tile (radius 28) on a slightly darker canvas; separate tiles with a 14px gap, never a border, rule or shadow.
- Do: Make each tile say one thing; give stats a huge numeral in the heading face with a small label.

#### Theme.swift (Locked)
Add this file to the target as is. Build every view from `Palette`, `Font.brand*`, `Metrics`, `Spacing`, `Motion`, `ThemeButtonStyle`, `themeSurface()`, `themeField()` and `themePage()`; never hard-code a color, size or duration. It covers the pressed, disabled, focus and error states; empty and loading follow the States table.
```swift
// Theme.swift: OpenText, Bento Grid. Generated by Looks Good; change the brief, not these values.
import SwiftUI

extension Color {
    init(hex: UInt32, opacity: Double = 1) {
        self.init(.sRGB, red: Double((hex >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255, green: Double((hex >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255, blue: Double(hex & 0xFF) / 255, opacity: opacity)
    }
    /// Adaptive color: follows the system light or dark appearance.
    init(light: UInt32, dark: UInt32, lightOpacity: Double = 1, darkOpacity: Double = 1) {
        #if canImport(UIKit)
        self = Color(UIColor { $0.userInterfaceStyle == .dark ? UIColor(Color(hex: dark, opacity: darkOpacity)) : UIColor(Color(hex: light, opacity: lightOpacity)) })
        #else
        self = Color(NSColor(name: nil) { $0.bestMatch(from: [.darkAqua, .aqua]) == .darkAqua ? NSColor(Color(hex: dark, opacity: darkOpacity)) : NSColor(Color(hex: light, opacity: lightOpacity)) })
        #endif
    }
}

enum Palette {
    static let canvas = Color(light: 0xEBECED, dark: 0x16191D)   // The screen background
    static let ink = Color(light: 0x16191D, dark: 0xF6F7F8)   // Default text and icons
    static let muted = Color(light: 0x66686B, dark: 0x9C9EA0)   // Secondary text from the palette
    static let line = Color(light: 0xCBCCCE, dark: 0x383A3E)   // Hairlines, dividers, resting input borders
    static let primary = Color(light: 0x5D7189, dark: 0x5D7189)   // Main action
    static let secondary = Color(light: 0xE3E8EE, dark: 0xE3E8EE)   // Support
    static let accent = Color(light: 0xCACD7E, dark: 0xCACD7E)   // One spark
    static let secTint = Color(light: 0xEAEBED, dark: 0x373A3E)   // Quiet secondary wash: selected row, tonal fills, hover on secondary it
    static let error = Color(light: 0x16191D, dark: 0xF6F7F8)   // Errors and destructive actions; never a stock red
    static let card = Color(light: 0xFBFBFB, dark: 0x25282C)   // Card and panel fill (paper); everything else sits on the canvas
    static let white = Color(light: 0xFFFFFF, dark: 0xFFFFFF)   // Pure white, where the UI kit draws it
    static let abPriPress = Color(light: 0x4C5D70, dark: 0x4C5D70)   // A colour of Bento Grid's UI kit
    static let kitPrimary = Color(light: 0xEBEBEB, dark: 0x34363A)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark
    static let kitPrimary-2 = Color(light: 0x505255, dark: 0xDCDDDF)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark
    static let kitSecondary = Color(light: 0xEBEBEB, dark: 0x1E2023)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark
    static let kitDestructive = Color(light: 0xC62F2F, dark: 0xFF7B72)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark
    static let abMuted = Color(light: 0x66686B, dark: 0xD2D3D5)   // A colour of Bento Grid's UI kit
    static let kitCheckbox = Color(light: 0x6D6F71, dark: 0xA7A8AA)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark
    static let kitDivider = Color(light: 0xE4E4E5, dark: 0x3A3D40)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark
    static let kitStepper = Color(light: 0xF0F0F0, dark: 0x2F3236)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark
    static let abTileLine = Color(light: 0x16191D, dark: 0xF6F7F8, lightOpacity: 0.08, darkOpacity: 0.1)   // A colour of Bento Grid's UI kit
    static let abTilePri = Color(light: 0xE5E8EB, dark: 0x30373F)   // A colour of Bento Grid's UI kit
    static let kitWheelText = Color(light: 0x666666, dark: 0xFFFFFF)   // Text of this component, kept at 4.5:1 on its fill in both modes
    static let kitWheelDateText = Color(light: 0x666666, dark: 0xFFFFFF)   // Text of this component, kept at 4.5:1 on its fill in both modes
    static let kitOverlayText = Color(light: 0x7F8183, dark: 0x6F6F70)   // Text of this component, kept at 4.5:1 on its fill in both modes
    static let kitTitleTile = Color(light: 0xCACD7E, dark: 0x000000, darkOpacity: 0)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark
    static let primaryText = Color(light: 0x596D84, dark: 0x74859A)   // The primary as text and small icons (text buttons, tinted icons), shad
    static let cardInk = Color(light: 0x16191D, dark: 0xF6F7F8)   // Titles and text on a card (`--kit-secondary` ground); the kit draws it
    static let cardMuted = Color(light: 0x66686B, dark: 0xD2D3D5)   // Secondary text on a card; the kit draws it as `--ab-muted`
    static let kitTabbarText = Color(light: 0x666666, dark: 0xFFFFFF)   // Named in this brief (a colour of the style)
    static let abPrimaryText = Color(light: 0x5D7189, dark: 0x6C7E94)   // Named in this brief (a colour of the style)
    static let onPrimary = Color(light: 0xFFFFFF, dark: 0xFFFFFF)   // text or icon on the fill
    static let onSecondary = Color(light: 0x141414, dark: 0x141414)   // text or icon on the fill
    static let onAccent = Color(light: 0x141414, dark: 0x141414)   // text or icon on the fill
    static let onError = Color(light: 0xEBECED, dark: 0x16191D)   // text or icon on the fill
}

extension Font {
    static let brandDisplay = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 34, relativeTo: .largeTitle)
    static let brandEyebrow = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 13, relativeTo: .caption)
    static let brandLabel = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 13, relativeTo: .caption)
    static let brandLargetitle = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 34, relativeTo: .largeTitle)
    static let brandTitle = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 28, relativeTo: .title)
    static let brandTitle2 = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 22, relativeTo: .title2)
    static let brandHeadline = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 17, relativeTo: .headline)
    static let brandBody = Font.custom("Geist-Regular", size: 17, relativeTo: .body)
    static let brandCallout = Font.custom("Geist-Regular", size: 16, relativeTo: .callout)
    static let brandSubhead = Font.custom("Geist-Regular", size: 15, relativeTo: .subheadline)
    static let brandFootnote = Font.custom("Geist-Regular", size: 13, relativeTo: .footnote)
    static let brandCaption = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 12, relativeTo: .caption)
    static let brandTablabel = Font.custom("Geist-SemiBold", size: 10, relativeTo: .caption2)
    // Display and Title: tracking -0.045em. Apply .textCase(.uppercase) and .tracking(...) at the call site.
}

enum Metrics {
    static let radiusControl: CGFloat = 16
    static let radiusField: CGFloat = 12
    static let radiusCard: CGFloat = 24
    static let outline: CGFloat = 1
    static let hit: CGFloat = 44   // minimum tappable size
    static let margin: CGFloat = 20   // side margin of every screen
    static let buttonHeight: CGFloat = 52, fieldHeight: CGFloat = 48, rowHeight: CGFloat = 52, cardPadding: CGFloat = 20
}

enum Spacing {
    static let xs: CGFloat = 4, s: CGFloat = 8, m: CGFloat = 16, l: CGFloat = 24, xl: CGFloat = 32
    static let sm: CGFloat = 12, ml: CGFloat = 20, xxl: CGFloat = 40, xxxl: CGFloat = 48
}

enum Motion {
    static let fast: Double = 0.12, base: Double = 0.18, slow: Double = 0.28
    static let press: Animation = .easeOut(duration: fast)
    static let settle: Animation = .easeOut(duration: base)
}

struct ThemeButtonStyle: ButtonStyle {
    @Environment(\.isEnabled) private var isEnabled
    @Environment(\.accessibilityReduceMotion) private var reduceMotion
    func makeBody(configuration: Configuration) -> some View {
        let shape = RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: Metrics.radiusControl, style: .continuous)
        let down = configuration.isPressed
        return configuration.label
            .font(.brandHeadline)
            .foregroundStyle(isEnabled ? Palette.onPrimary : Palette.muted)
            .padding(.horizontal, Spacing.l)
            .frame(minHeight: Metrics.hit)
            .background(isEnabled ? Palette.primary : Palette.line, in: shape)
            .opacity(down ? 0.85 : 1)
            .animation(reduceMotion ? nil : Motion.press, value: down)
    }
}

struct ThemeSurface: ViewModifier {
    func body(content: Content) -> some View {
        let shape = RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: Metrics.radiusCard, style: .continuous)
        return content
            .padding(Spacing.m)
            .background(Palette.card, in: shape)
            .overlay(shape.strokeBorder(Palette.line, lineWidth: Metrics.outline))
    }
}

struct ThemeField: ViewModifier {
    var focused = false
    var failed = false
    func body(content: Content) -> some View {
        let shape = RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: Metrics.radiusField, style: .continuous)
        return content
            .font(.brandBody)
            .padding(.horizontal, Spacing.m)
            .frame(minHeight: Metrics.hit)
            .background(Palette.canvas, in: shape)
            .overlay(shape.strokeBorder(failed ? Palette.error : Palette.line, lineWidth: failed ? 2 : Metrics.outline))
            .overlay(shape.inset(by: -4).stroke(Palette.primary, lineWidth: focused ? 3 : 0))
    }
}

extension View {
    func themeSurface() -> some View { modifier(ThemeSurface()) }
    func themeField(focused: Bool = false, failed: Bool = false) -> some View { modifier(ThemeField(focused: focused, failed: failed)) }
    /// Page background and default ink; put it on the root view.
    func themePage() -> some View { background(Palette.canvas.ignoresSafeArea()).foregroundStyle(Palette.ink) }
}
```

#### App icon (Locked)
Full-bleed `#5d7189` tile with one simple glyph (or the brand initial in the heading face) in `#ffffff`; flat, no shadow, no gradient. Deliver a 1024x1024 master without transparency or pre-rounded corners (the system masks it), a dark variant on `#16191d` and a tinted variant (glyph only, one color), and derive every other size from the master.

## Part 2: Design tokens

These are the brand's tokens: use them as given, and use nothing else for colour, type or spacing. The first block is the same file "Save + files" writes; the last block holds the values of every custom property the Part 3 CSS reads, resolved on the kit root, so every `var(--x)` in Part 3 has a value here.

```swift
struct ThemeButtonStyle: ButtonStyle {

    @Environment(\.accessibilityReduceMotion) private var reduceMotion
```

### Resolved custom properties read by the kit CSS

Set these on the kit root (the element carrying class `ab sb-ab`) before the Part 3 CSS applies. The first block is the light look; the second lists only the values that differ in dark, scoped by `data-mode`.

```css
.ab.sb-ab {
  --ab-accent: #cacd7e;
  --ab-accent-ink: #16191d;
  --ab-accent-mark: #cacd7e;
  --ab-canvas: #ebeced;
  --ab-display: 1;
  --ab-font-b: "Geist", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --ab-font-h: "Geist", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --ab-font-label: "Geist", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --ab-font-ui: "Geist", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --ab-hl-scale: 1;
  --ab-hs: 1;
  --ab-ink: #16191d;
  --ab-line: #cbccce;
  --ab-muted: #66686b;
  --ab-on-accent: #141414;
  --ab-on-pri-press: #ffffff;
  --ab-on-primary: #ffffff;
  --ab-on-secondary: #141414;
  --ab-pri-press: #4c5d70;
  --ab-pri-tint: #dadde1;
  --ab-primary: #5d7189;
  --ab-primary-text: #5d7189;
  --ab-sec-tint: #eaebed;
  --ab-secondary: #e3e8ee;
  --ab-sub: #f1f1f1;
  --ab-tile: #fbfbfb;
  --ab-tile-line: rgba(22,25,29,0.08);
  --ab-tile-pri: #e5e8eb;
  --ab-tile-sec: #f6f7f8;
  --ab-tr: 1;
  --bn-c: color-mix(in srgb, #16191d 7%, #fbfbfb);
  --bn-gap: clamp(10px, 1.5cqw, 16px);
  --bn-off: color-mix(in srgb, #16191d 62%, #fbfbfb);
  --bn-pt: color-mix(in srgb, #5d7189 62%, #16191d);
  --bn-r: clamp(22px, 3.1cqw, 28px);
  --bn-ring: #5d7189;

  --bn-well: color-mix(in srgb, #16191d 7%, #fbfbfb);
  --bnk-float: 0 0 0 1px rgba(22,25,29,0.08);

  --k-field: 48px;

  --k-footnote-lh: 18px;
  --k-gap: 28px;
  --k-headline: 17px;
  --k-headline-lh: 22px;
  --k-info: #5d7189;
  --k-info-ico: #5d7189;
  --k-large-title: 34px;
  --k-large-title-lh: 41px;
  --k-margin: 20px;
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, #16191d 28%, #66686b);
  --k-ok: #e3e8ee;
  --k-ok-ico: #7d8186;
  --k-pri-on-tint: #4f5f73;
  --k-pri-text: #596d84;
  --k-r: min(16px, 16px);
  --k-row: 52px;
  --k-sheet-r: 28px;
  --k-sheet-shadow: none;
  --k-subhead: 15px;
  --k-subhead-lh: 20px;
  --k-tab: 83px;
  --k-tab-label: 10px;
  --k-tab-label-lh: 12px;
  --k-tap: 44px;
  --k-title: 28px;
  --k-title-2: 22px;
  --k-title-2-lh: 28px;
  --k-title-lh: 34px;
  --k-ui: Geist, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --k-warn: #cacd7e;
  --k-warn-ico: #797c52;
  --sb-card-r: 24px;
  --sb-danger: #16191d;
  --sb-danger-on: #ebeced;
  --sb-danger-text: #16191d;
  --sb-focus-auto: #5d7189;
  --sb-focus-offset: 3px;
  --sb-focus-w: 3px;
  --sb-r: 16px;
  --sb-ui-ff: Geist, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --sb-ui-ls: -0.17px;
  --sb-ui-tt: none;
}
```

```css
.ab.sb-ab[data-mode="dark"] {
  --ab-accent-ink: #cacd7e;
  --ab-accent-mark: transparent;
  --ab-canvas: #16191d;
  --ab-ink: #f6f7f8;
  --ab-line: #383a3e;
  --ab-muted: #d2d3d5;
  --ab-pri-tint: #1f242a;
  --ab-primary-text: #6c7e94;
  --ab-sec-tint: #373a3e;
  --ab-sub: #1e2023;
  --ab-tile: #25282c;
  --ab-tile-line: rgba(246,247,248,0.1);
  --ab-tile-pri: #30373f;
  --ab-tile-sec: #53565b;
  --bn-c: #1e2023;
  --bn-off: color-mix(in srgb, #f6f7f8 62%, #25282c);
  --bn-pt: color-mix(in srgb, #5d7189 48%, #f6f7f8);
  --bn-s: #1e2023;
  --bn-well: #1e2023;
  --bnk-float: 0 0 0 1px rgba(246,247,248,0.1);
  --k-err: #f6f7f8;
  --k-err-ico: #f6f7f8;
  --k-info-ico: #65788f;
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, #f6f7f8 28%, #d2d3d5);
  --k-ok-ico: #e3e8ee;
  --k-pri-on-tint: #7c8c9f;
  --k-pri-text: #74859a;
  --k-warn-ico: #cacd7e;
  --sb-danger: #f6f7f8;
  --sb-danger-on: #16191d;
  --sb-danger-text: #f6f7f8;
}
```

Set per element (in the markup's inline `style` or by a rule inside `kit.css`, not on the root): `--h`, `--k-inset`, `--k-picked`, `--pr`, `--t`, `--tone`, `--tone-ico`, `--v`, `--wr`.

Optional hooks this style does not set (each `var()` in `kit.css` carries its own fallback, so leave them unset): `--k-callout-shadow`, `--k-dis-label`, `--sb-focus`.

## Part 3: UI kit source

First comes `kit.css`: every CSS rule that draws the UI kit in this style, in cascade order, grouped by the file it came from. Then each component, with what it is for, its iOS treatment, its states and the reference markup of every state. Port the CSS values exactly; treat the markup as illustrative.

### Kit root

Every selector in `kit.css` is scoped to this root. Rename or fold the scope into your own, but keep the same attributes' meaning (`data-ae` is the style, `data-mode` is light or dark).

```html
<section class="ab sb-ab sb-window sb-kit" data-ae="bento-grid" data-mode="light" data-accent-text="low" data-hero="split" data-palette="chips" data-sections="stacked" data-highlight="ink" data-states="button" data-subcopy="on" data-hcls="neo-grotesk" data-bcls="neo-grotesk" data-sb-danger="ink" data-k-field="box">
  <!-- a component's reference markup goes here -->
</section>
```

### kit.css

801 rules from 4 files: `baseline`, `css/aesthetic-board.css`, `css/aesthetics/bento-grid.css`, `css/see-built.css`.

```css
/* ===== baseline (from css/styles.css) ===== */
*,
::before,
::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  ::before,
  ::after {
    animation-duration: 0.001ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.001ms !important;
  }
}

/* ===== css/aesthetic-board.css ===== */
.ab {
  container: ab / inline-size;
  position: relative;
  isolation: isolate;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b));
  --ab-pad: clamp(20px, 4.4cqw, 44px);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 400;
  line-height: 1.5;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
  text-align: left;
  border-radius: 20px;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  font-synthesis: none;
}

.ab *,
.ab ::before,
.ab ::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

:where(.ab) button,
:where(.ab) input {
  font: inherit;
  color: inherit;
}

.ab h2,
.ab h3,
.ab p,
.ab ul {
  margin: 0px;
  padding: 0px;
}

.ab i {
  font-style: normal;
}

.ab-brand {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  min-width: 0px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  font-size: clamp(15px, 2cqw, 19px);
}

.ab-wordmark {
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab:not(.ab-slide) .ab-wordmark {
  overflow: visible;
  text-overflow: clip;
  white-space: normal;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}

.ab-mark {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  font: 700 14px/1 var(--ab-font-h);
}

.ab-rule {
  height: 1px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
}

.ab-label,
.ab-fieldlabel {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
}

.ab-labelrow {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.ab-label {
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab-headline {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  font-size: calc(clamp(32px, 7.4cqw, 70px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  text-wrap: balance;
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab[data-ae] .ab-headline:not([data-wrap-last-resort]) {
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab-hl-glue {
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab-subcopy {
  max-width: 36em;
  text-wrap: pretty;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: clamp(13.5px, 1.5cqw, 16px);
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin-top: clamp(14px, 2cqw, 22px) !important;
}

.ab:where([data-highlight="ink"]) .ab-hl {
  color: var(--ab-accent-ink);
  background-image: linear-gradient(transparent 62%, var(--ab-accent-mark, transparent) 62%, var(--ab-accent-mark, transparent) 94%, transparent 94%);
  -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;
  box-decoration-break: clone;
}

.ab-card {
  padding: clamp(18px, 2.8cqw, 26px);
  border-radius: 16px;
}

.ab-seg {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 5px;
  border-radius: 12px;
}

.ab-seg-btn {
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-height: 36px;
  padding: 8px 6px;
  border: 0px;
  background: transparent;
  border-radius: 9px;
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab-card-title {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: clamp(21px, 2.7cqw, 27px);
  line-height: 1.12;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  margin: 24px 0px 8px !important;
}

.ab-card-body {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin-bottom: 22px !important;
}

.ab-cta {
  width: 100%;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 13px 16px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  font-size: 13.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab-cta-arrow::before {
  content: "↗";
}

.ab-fieldlabel {
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.ab .ab-input {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
  padding: 13px 15px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: transparent;
  color: inherit;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: inherit;
  text-transform: none;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  outline: none;
}

.ab[data-ae] .ab-input:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.ab-toggle {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: 44px;
  font-size: 13px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ab-switch {
  appearance: none;
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  margin: 0px;
  width: 50px;
  height: 29px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: var(--ab-line);
  transition: background 0.18s;
}

.ab-switch::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 4px;
  top: 4px;
  width: 21px;
  height: 21px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  transition: transform 0.18s;
}

.ab-switch:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab-switch:checked::before {
  transform: translateX(21px);
}

.ab-list {
  list-style: none;
}

.ab-row {
  display: grid;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px clamp(10px, 2cqw, 20px);
  padding: 13px 2px;
  grid-template-columns: 2.6ch minmax(0px, 1fr) auto minmax(60px, 150px) 4.5ch;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  font-size: 13px;
}

.ab-row:last-child {
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.ab-row-bar {
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.ab-row-bar i {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  background: var(--ab-secondary);
}

.ab-fit {
  display: inline-block;
  padding: 2px 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.5;
  white-space: nowrap;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab-fit[data-fit="match"] {
  background: var(--ab-sec-tint);
  border-color: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab-type-apply {
  padding: 10px 16px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab-type-apply:hover {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
}

.ab-type-apply:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

@container ab (max-width: 600px) {
  .ab-headline {
    font-size: calc(clamp(30px, 10.5cqw, 52px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  }

  .ab-brand {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
  }

  .ab-labelrow {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    row-gap: 4px;
  }

  .ab-labelrow > .ab-label:last-child:not(:first-child) {
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  .ab-row {
    grid-template-columns: 2.6ch minmax(0px, 1fr) 4.5ch;
    row-gap: 6px;
  }

  .ab-row-bar {
    display: none;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .ab *,
  .ab ::before,
  .ab ::after {
    transition: none !important;
    animation: auto ease 0s 1 normal none running none !important;
  }
}

/* ===== css/aesthetics/bento-grid.css ===== */
.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] {
  --bn-r: clamp(22px, 3.1cqw, 28px);
  --bn-gap: clamp(10px, 1.5cqw, 16px);
  border-radius: 26px;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-rule {
  display: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] :is(.ab-hero-copy, .ab-art, .ab-palette, .ab-type, .ab-seg, .ab-card-title, .ab-card-body, .ab-input, .ab-toggle, .ab-state, .ab-row) {
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  border-radius: var(--bn-r);
  box-shadow: none;
  transition: transform 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.7, 0.2, 1);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] :is(.ab-hero-copy, .ab-art, .ab-palette, .ab-type, .ab-card-title, .ab-card-body, .ab-state, .ab-row):hover {
  transform: scale(1.01);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-mark {
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-brand {
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.03em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-headline {
  margin: auto 0px;
  padding: clamp(18px, 3cqw, 34px) 0px;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.045em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-hl {
  color: var(--ab-primary-text);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-subcopy {
  max-width: 24em;
  font-size: clamp(14px, 1.55cqw, 17px);
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-labelrow {
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-label {
  font-size: 12px;
  letter-spacing: 0.01em;
  text-transform: none;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-fit {
  border: 0px;
  background: var(--ab-sub);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-fit[data-fit="match"] {
  background: var(--ab-tile-pri);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-type-apply {
  padding: 12px 20px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  transition: transform 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-type-apply:hover {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0px, 1fr) clamp(150px, 22cqw, 230px) clamp(170px, 26cqw, 250px);
  grid-template-rows: auto minmax(0px, 1fr) auto auto;
  gap: var(--bn-gap);
  min-height: clamp(250px, 30cqw, 320px);
  padding: 0px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card::before {
  content: "";
  grid-area: 1 / 1 / 5 / 2;
  z-index: 0;
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  border-radius: var(--bn-r);
  box-shadow: none;
  transition: transform 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.7, 0.2, 1);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card:hover::before {
  transform: scale(1.01);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card::after {
  content: "";
  grid-area: 1 / 2 / 5 / 3;
  z-index: 0;
  border-radius: var(--bn-r);
  min-height: 100%;
  background: radial-gradient(circle closest-side, color-mix(in srgb, #ffffff 55%, transparent) 0, transparent 100%) 36% 30% / 28% 28% no-repeat,
    radial-gradient(circle closest-side at 42% 40%, color-mix(in srgb, #ffffff 22%, var(--ab-primary)) 0, var(--ab-primary) 45%, color-mix(in srgb, #000000 40%, var(--ab-primary)) 96%, transparent 100%) 50% 40% / 74% 74% no-repeat,
    radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 50%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 22%, transparent) 0, transparent 68%) 50% 88% / 70% 16% no-repeat,
    var(--ab-tile-sec);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card > * {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-seg {
  grid-area: 1 / 1;
  justify-self: start;
  width: auto;
  margin: 22px 22px 0px;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 5px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--ab-sub);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-seg-btn {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  min-height: 36px;
  padding: 0px 16px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  transition: background 0.18s, color 0.18s;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-seg-btn:hover {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-title,
.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-title:hover {
  grid-area: 3 / 1;
  padding: 18px 22px 0px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: none;
  transform: none;
  font-size: clamp(32px, 3.6cqw, 40px);
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.045em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-body,
.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-body:hover {
  grid-area: 4 / 1;
  align-self: start;
  padding: 6px 22px 22px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: none;
  transform: none;
  font-size: clamp(13px, 1.5cqw, 16px);
  line-height: 1.4;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-cta {
  grid-area: 1 / 3 / 5 / 4;
  width: auto;
  min-height: 100%;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-rows: auto 1fr;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0px, 1fr);
  place-items: start stretch;
  padding: 22px;
  border-radius: var(--bn-r);
  text-align: left;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  box-shadow: none;
  transition: filter 0.18s, transform 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.7, 0.2, 1);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-cta-arrow {
  grid-row: 1;
  justify-self: end;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-cta-label {
  grid-row: 2;
  place-self: end start;
  font-size: 22px;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.1;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-cta:hover {
  filter: brightness(1.07);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-cta:active {
  transform: scale(0.99);
  filter: brightness(0.94);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-cta-arrow::before {
  content: "↗";
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  font-size: 19px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-on-primary) 18%, transparent);
  transition: transform 0.2s;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-cta:hover .ab-cta-arrow::before {
  transform: translate(2px, -2px);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-fieldlabel {
  grid-area: 1 / 1 / 2 / 5;
  z-index: 1;
  place-self: start;
  margin: 14px 18px 0px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-input {
  grid-area: 1 / 1 / 2 / 5;
  margin: 0px;
  padding: 30px clamp(18px, 2.6cqw, 28px) clamp(16px, 2.2cqw, 24px);
  font-size: clamp(17px, 2.1cqw, 23px);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  outline: none;
  transition: box-shadow 0.2s;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-input:focus {
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-toggle {
  grid-area: 1 / 5 / 2 / 7;
  min-height: 0px;
  padding: 0px clamp(16px, 2.2cqw, 26px) 26px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-switch {
  width: 52px;
  height: 30px;
  border-radius: 999px;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-switch::before {
  left: 3px;
  top: 3px;
  width: 24px;
  height: 24px;
  background: rgb(255, 255, 255);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25) 0px 1px 3px;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-switch:checked::before {
  transform: translateX(22px);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-list {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0px, 1fr));
  gap: var(--bn-gap);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template: "pct pct" ". ." 1fr "name name" "status status" "bar bar" / minmax(0px, 1fr) auto;
  gap: 0px 10px;
  min-height: clamp(190px, 23cqw, 250px);
  padding: clamp(18px, 2.4cqw, 28px);
  border: 0px;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-row:last-child {
  border: 0px;
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-row:nth-child(1) {
  grid-column: span 2;
  background: var(--ab-sec-tint);
  grid-template-rows: auto 1fr auto auto;
  grid-template-areas: "pct status" ". ." "name name" "bar bar";
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-row-bar {
  grid-area: bar;
  height: 8px;
  margin-top: 16px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 9%, transparent);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-row-bar i {
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--ab-secondary);
}

.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-seg-btn:focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-cta:focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-switch:focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-type-apply:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-primary-text);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}

@container ab (max-width: 600px) {
  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card::after {
    display: none;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-headline {
    margin: 0px;
    padding: 22px 0px 18px;
    font-size: calc(clamp(34px, 12.4cqw, 54px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0px, 1fr);
    grid-template-rows: auto auto auto auto;
    min-height: 0px;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card::before {
    grid-area: 1 / 1 / 4 / 2;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-cta {
    grid-area: 4 / 1 / 5 / 2;
    min-height: 0px;
    aspect-ratio: 2 / 1;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-title {
    padding-top: 22px;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-title,
  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-title:hover {
    grid-area: 2 / 1;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-body,
  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-card .ab-card-body:hover {
    grid-area: 3 / 1;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-fieldlabel,
  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-input {
    grid-area: 1 / 1 / 2 / -1;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-toggle {
    grid-area: 2 / 1 / 3 / -1;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-toggle {
    min-height: 72px;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-list {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0px, 1fr));
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-row {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0px, 1fr);
    grid-template-areas: "pct" "." "name" "status" "bar";
    min-height: 190px;
    padding: 18px;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-row-bar {
    display: block;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-row:nth-child(1) {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
    min-height: 200px;
    grid-template-areas: "pct" "." "name" "status" "bar";
    grid-template-rows: auto 1fr auto auto auto;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] :is(.ab-hero-copy, .ab-art, .ab-palette, .ab-type, .ab-card-title, .ab-card-body, .ab-state, .ab-row):hover,
  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-cta:hover,
  .ab[data-ae="bento-grid"] .ab-cta:active {
    transform: none;
  }
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-card-title:not(.ab-card .ab-card-title),
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-card-title:not(.ab-card .ab-card-title):hover {
  padding: 0px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: none;
  transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-card .ab-cta {
  min-height: var(--k-btn, 52px);
  aspect-ratio: auto;
  padding: 0px 6px 0px 20px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-cta-label {
  place-self: center auto;
  font-size: inherit;
  line-height: inherit;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-cta-arrow {

}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit :is(.ab-cta.sb-k-btn, .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2, .sb-k-danger) {

}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-btn {
  padding-left: 20px;
  padding-right: 6px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-cta:hover {
  transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-list.sb-k-list {
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  display: flex !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row:nth-child(1) {
  border-radius: 20px;
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  padding: 10px 16px !important;
  min-height: var(--k-row, 52px) !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row:hover {
  transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-row .sb-k-ico {

}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-toggle.sb-tog {

  border-radius: 20px;

}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row.is-picked {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-scr[data-scr="form"] .ab-toggle.sb-tog {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-ph {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-scrim {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-pop-arrow {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-fab {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-link {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-link.sb-k-pressed {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-icon {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-icon svg {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-icon span {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-combo-in {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit mark.sb-k-match {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-stepper {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-step:active {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-step-val {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-knob-dial {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-knob-ind {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-knob-val {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-date-ico {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-sel,
.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-start,

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-wheel {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-overlaybox {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-sheetbox {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-dialog {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-dialog-t {

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-side {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-drawer {

  border-right-style:;
  border-right-width:;
  border-bottom-color:;
  border-bottom-style:;
  border-bottom-width:;
  border-left-color:;
  border-left-style:;
  border-left-width:;
  border-image-source:;
  border-image-slice:;
  border-image-width:;
  border-image-outset:;
  border-image-repeat:;

@keyframes sbk-bento-spin {

}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="bento-grid"], [data-ae="bento-grid--classic"]).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-split-more, .sb-k-fab, .sb-k-backtop, .sb-k-step, .sb-k-day, .sb-k-lb-close, .sb-k-lb-prev, .sb-k-lb-next, .sb-k-plain, .sb-k-nm-item, .sb-k-nb-links a, .sb-k-crumbs a, .sb-k-anchors a, .sb-k-menubar button, .sb-k-rb-btn, .sb-k-pie-item, .sb-k-acc-sum, .sb-k-link, .sb-k-mega-col a):focus-visible {

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit {
  --bn-well: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 7%, var(--ab-tile));
  --bn-s: var(--ab-tile);
  --bn-c: var(--ab-tile);
  --bn-ring: var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  --bn-pt: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary) 62%, var(--ab-ink));
  --bn-off: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 62%, var(--ab-tile));
  --sb-focus-w: 3px;
  --sb-focus-offset: 3px;
  --k-list-box: card;
  --k-callout-edge-w: 0px;
  --k-callout-r: 20px;
  --k-callout-fill: 16%;
  --k-sheet-r: 28px;
  --k-sheet-shadow: none;
  --k-arrow-edge: 0px;
  --k-pri-text: var(--bn-pt);
  --k-pri-on-tint: var(--bn-pt);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit[data-mode="dark"] {
  --bn-well: var(--ab-sub);
  --bn-pt: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary) 48%, var(--ab-ink));
  --bn-off: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 62%, var(--ab-tile));
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-cell :is(.sb-k-card, .sb-k-pop, .sb-k-listbox, .sb-k-cmdmenu, .sb-k-menu, .sb-k-cal, .sb-k-wheel, .sb-k-acc, .sb-k-mx-tile, .sb-k-car-card, .sb-k-ribbon, .sb-k-menubar, .sb-k-navbar, .sb-k-tabbarbox, .sb-k-mega-panel, .sb-k-scrollbox, .sb-k-nm-children, .sb-k-ctxbox, .sb-k-popbox, .sb-k-pie, .sb-k-fabbox) {
  --bn-c: var(--ab-tile);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-layout :is(.sb-k-card, .sb-k-pop, .sb-k-listbox, .sb-k-cmdmenu, .sb-k-menu, .sb-k-cal, .sb-k-wheel, .sb-k-acc, .sb-k-mx-tile, .sb-k-car-card, .sb-k-ribbon, .sb-k-menubar, .sb-k-navbar, .sb-k-tabbarbox, .sb-k-mega-panel, .sb-k-scrollbox, .sb-k-nm-children, .sb-k-ctxbox, .sb-k-popbox, .sb-k-pie, .sb-k-fabbox) {
  --bn-c: var(--bn-well);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit :is(.sb-k-pressed, .is-pressed) {

}

}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-focus {

}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-field.is-focus,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-search.is-focus {

}

  transform: none;

}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-fab {

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-link {

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-menu li[aria-selected="true"],
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-open {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-menu li[aria-selected="true"] :is(.sb-ico, .sb-k-kbd),
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-open :is(.sb-ico, .sb-k-kbd) {
  color: inherit;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-menu li:not([aria-selected="true"]):not(.is-open):hover {
  background: var(--bn-c);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-menu .sb-ico {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-danger,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-danger .sb-ico {
  color: var(--sb-danger-text, var(--sb-danger));
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.sb-k-menu-sep {
  height: 0px;
  min-height: 0px;
  margin: 4px 0px;
  padding: 0px;
  background: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.sb-k-menu-sep + li {
  margin-top: 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-combo .sb-k-menu {
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  margin: 8px -8px 6px !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit mark.sb-k-match {
  padding: 0px 3px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-listbox {
  padding: 8px;
  border-radius: 22px;
  background: var(--bn-s);
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-listbox li[aria-selected="true"] .sb-k-box:checked {
  background: var(--ab-on-primary);
  border-color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-listbox li[aria-selected="true"] .sb-k-box[type="checkbox"]:checked::after {
  border-color: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-check {
  gap: 14px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-box {
  width: 26px;
  height: 26px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--bn-off);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-box[type="radio"] {
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-box:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-box[type="checkbox"]:checked::after {
  width: 13px;
  height: 7px;
  border-color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  border-width: 0px 0px 3px 3px;
  border-style: solid;
  border-radius: 1px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-box[type="radio"]:checked {
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 8px var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-box[type="radio"]:checked::after {
  content: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-box:disabled,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-switch:disabled {
  opacity: 1;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-box:checked:disabled {
  background: var(--bn-off);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-check.is-disabled {
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-toggle.sb-tog {
  padding: 10px 14px 10px 18px;
  border-radius: 18px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-switch {
  width: 56px;
  height: 32px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--bn-off);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-switch::before {
  left: 5px;
  top: 5px;
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--bn-off);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-switch:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-switch:checked::before {
  left: 5px;
  top: 5px;
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  background: var(--ab-on-primary);
  transform: translateX(24px);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-switch:disabled {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 7%, var(--ab-tile));
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-switch:checked:disabled {
  background: var(--bn-off);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-toggle.is-disabled {
  color: var(--k-muted);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 5%, var(--ab-tile));
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-slider-h {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-slider-h span:last-child {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-size: 20px;
  line-height: 20px;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit input.sb-k-range::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
  height: 14px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ab-primary) var(--v, 50%), var(--bn-c) var(--v, 50%));
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit input.sb-k-range::-webkit-slider-thumb {
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  margin-top: -8px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 11px;
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 4px var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-stepwrap {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-stepper {
  gap: 6px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-step {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-step:active {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-step:disabled {
  opacity: 1;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 5%, var(--ab-tile)) !important;
  color: var(--k-muted) !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-step-val {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  min-width: 56px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-size: 30px;
  line-height: 44px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.05em;
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-arc {
  mask: radial-gradient(farthest-side, transparent calc(100% - 8px), rgb(0, 0, 0) calc(100% - 7px));
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-dial {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 18px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-ind {
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  width: 4px;
  margin-left: -2px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-txt b {
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-val {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-size: 26px;
  line-height: 28px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-seg {
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 0px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-seg-btn {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  transition: transform 0.15s, background-color 0.15s;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-seg-btn:active {
  transform: scale(0.96);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs {
  gap: 6px;
  border-bottom: 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"] {
  padding: 0px 18px;
  border-radius: 14px;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  text-transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"]:not([aria-selected="true"]) {
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"]::after {
  content: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-card,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-car-card {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 24px;
  background: var(--bn-s);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-card .ab-card-title {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
  text-transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-card .ab-card-body {
  font-weight: 400;
  letter-spacing: -0.005em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-list.sb-k-list {
  gap: 6px !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row:nth-child(1) {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  background: var(--bn-s);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row.is-picked,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row.is-picked:nth-child(1) {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  --k-muted: var(--ab-on-primary);
  --ab-muted: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row.is-picked :is(.sb-k-title, .sb-k-sub, .sb-k-trail, .sb-ico, .sb-k-chev) {
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-title {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.015em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-sub {
  letter-spacing: -0.005em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-trail {
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-ico {
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-av {
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: var(--ab-secondary);
  color: var(--ab-on-secondary);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  text-transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-av-s {
  border-radius: 11px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-av-l {
  border-radius: 20px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-avs .sb-k-av {
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px var(--bn-s) !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-badge,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-count {
  min-height: 26px;
  min-width: 26px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-count {
  background: var(--bn-c);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-badge,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-count.is-accent {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-fit {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-fit[data-fit="match"] {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-dotwrap .sb-badge {
  border-radius: 4px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px var(--bn-s);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row-bar.sb-k-bar {
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  box-shadow: none;
  overflow: hidden;
  height: 14px !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row-bar.sb-k-bar i {
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-progress .sb-k-slider-h span:last-child {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-size: 20px;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-callout {
  padding: 16px 18px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  box-shadow: none;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  letter-spacing: -0.005em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-callout[data-tone] {
  border: 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-callout > .sb-ico {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-callout b {
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.015em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-callout[data-tone="error"] {
  box-shadow: inset 5px 0 0 var(--sb-danger);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-toast {
  border-radius: 18px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  box-shadow: none;
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-toast button {
  margin: 4px 0px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 16%, transparent);
  text-decoration: none;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-empty {
  padding: 26px 16px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-emptyico {
  width: 64px;
  height: 64px;
  border-radius: 22px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-empty-t {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
  text-transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-sheet {
  padding-top: 14px;
  border-radius: 28px 28px 0px 0px;
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-grab {
  width: 44px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 22%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-sheet-t {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
  text-transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-divider .ab-rule {
  display: block;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 10%, var(--ab-tile));
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-section .ab-label {
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  text-transform: none;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-ph {
  border-radius: 16px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-dots {
  gap: 6px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-dots i {
  width: 8px;
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 18%, transparent);
  transition: width 0.2s;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-dots i.is-on {
  width: 26px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-mx-tile {
  padding: 8px 8px 12px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  background: var(--bn-s);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-mx-tile .sb-k-ph {
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-mx-label .ab-label,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-mx-row > :first-child:not(.sb-k-mx-track) {
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  background: var(--bn-s);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc + .sb-k-acc {
  margin-top: 6px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-acc-sum {
  border-radius: 20px;
  background: transparent;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc-chev {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border-radius: 11px;
  background: var(--bn-c);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  transition: background-color 0.15s;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc[open] .sb-k-acc-chev {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  transform: rotate(180deg);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc[open] > .sb-k-acc-sum {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc-body {
  border-top: 0px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  letter-spacing: -0.005em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-overlaybox,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-sheetbox,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-lightbox {
  border-radius: 24px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-sheetbox {
  background: var(--ab-tile);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-scrim,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-sheetbox::before {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 52%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-dialog {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 28px;
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-dialog-a {
  gap: 6px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="bento-grid"].sb-kit .sb-k-side {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 28px 0px 0px 28px;
  background: var(--ab-tile);
  box-shadow: none;
}
```
